# AI Media Scout — 2026-10-24

Expanded discovery covers 66 creative fields, 531 repository queries and 18 model tasks, with live primary-source research across all fields and eight web ecosystems. Today’s complete guides cover local image/video/audio work, editable CAD, neural scenes, interactive interfaces, score notation and agent-assisted publishing. Earlier screened entries receive full guides where supported; first library coverage is not presented as a product launch. All selected original software grants received complete-text review. Models, assets, required hosts and service costs remain separate. These are documentation/source reviews, with no app installation or hands-on testing. Quality remains unverified except where current, cited prototype or unfinished-work evidence supports Experimental. A newly found inherited-code license conflict is flagged on Ruach Studio’s existing library entry. Codeberg and SourceHut access gaps and all repository query failures, incomplete results and pagination bounds remain visible. Weekly whole-library review begins October 26 and is not due today. All 531 repository queries and 18 model tasks were attempted: 530 repository queries succeeded, one failed, and 113 did not retrieve all reported matches. This is broad, bounded discovery, not an exhaustive census.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

## Coverage

| Field | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| Images & design | Researched local generation and layered editing. Scumble and Kokon provide distinct local/API routes; Kokon’s default Qwen-Image 2.1 has noncommercial model restrictions despite open app code. [Source 1](https://www.denrakeiw.com/scumble) [Source 2](https://kokon.app/) [Source 3](https://autoshade.dev/) |
| Video, animation & film | Covered agent-assisted edits, transcript cuts, motion templates and long-form production. New guides distinguish real model operations from mock previews and external service dependencies. [Source 1](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo) [Source 2](https://github.com/MakeMyClip/editor) |
| Audio, music & voice | Reviewed local speech, ambient music and sound-library analysis. LatentScore’s default combines learned retrieval with procedural synthesis; its optional experimental backend is a separate scope. [Source 1](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 2](https://app.danghuuson.com/vieneu-audio-studio/) |
| 3D, reconstruction & assets | Covered generated assets, neural surfaces, splats and editable geometry. Current source review separates image-to-3D demonstrations from independent usability and physical print validation. [Source 1](https://lichtfeld.io/) [Source 2](https://meshxl.github.io/) [Source 3](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/) |
| Browser tools & web media | Browser creative systems and agent-backed authoring remain in scope. A browser interface does not establish that the inference backend works on every host. [Source 1](https://nodetool.ai/) [Source 2](https://yuyou-dev.github.io/OpenHome3D/) |
| WebXR, VR & AR | Searched immersive-web creation and depth-derived stereo. TransferThought and Allegory XR supplied adjacent primary context, but open-source AI eligibility was not established for a new entry from their product claims alone. [Source 1](https://www.transferthought.com/) [Source 2](https://www.allegoryxr.com/) |
| Computational art & creative coding | Reviewed artist/developer workflows that combine neural models with editable parameters, including vector generation and sound synthesis. Conventional procedural software is not included merely because AI helped write it. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06494) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) |
| Interactive, immersive & live media | Researched live agent-controlled canvases, gesture interfaces and interactive media construction. Air Pen Studio and Airborne Buddy received full guides for concrete MediaPipe-powered drawing and character control; target-camera performance is unverified. [Source 1](https://nodetool.ai/) [Source 2](https://ghostarcade.live/) [Source 3](https://air-pen-studio-adsouza978.netlify.app) |
| 3D printing & generative CAD | Covered agent-driven CAD and generated geometry. Partwright’s noncommercial software license excludes it; other tools’ STEP/STL exports still require design and fabrication checks. [Source 1](https://agentcad.dev/) [Source 2](https://shylenko.com/projects/kernelcad/) [Source 3](https://github.com/kemper/partwright) |
| Games & production pipelines | Searched AI-assisted engine workflows and asset pipelines. Cambrian’s primary site and Unity/Blender integrations were inspected for concrete authoring uses; engine and model terms remain separate. [Source 1](https://cambrianengine.com/) [Source 2](https://github.com/Besty0728/Unity-Skills) |
| Motion capture & character animation | Researched motion generation, rigging and agent-edited timelines. AI4AnimationPy’s current code grant is noncommercial, so popularity or motion demos do not make it eligible under this project’s software policy. [Source 1](https://facebookresearch.github.io/ai4animationpy/) [Source 2](https://github.com/facebookresearch/ai4animationpy) |
| Avatars, digital humans & lip sync | Covered talking heads, tutors and signing avatars. GMTalker and VTutor supplied primary research/model context; each implementation still needs its own complete software and checkpoint review. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/calyi/GMTalker) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04103) |
| VFX, compositing & relighting | Searched neural relighting, rendering cleanup and effect generation. UniRelight and neural inverse-rendering materials were researched; model access and restrictions are not inherited from another repository’s root license. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/nvidia/UniRelight) [Source 2](https://neural-pbir.github.io/) |
| Spatial audio & volumetric media | Reviewed neural scene and room-acoustic representations for immersive media. Research demonstrations are not treated as turnkey spatial authoring apps. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.02809) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03036) |
| Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering | Covered posed-image reconstruction and Gaussian capture. The new OpenReco guide specifically distinguishes the unvalidated neural branch from its demonstrated conventional mapping pipeline. [Source 1](https://lichtfeld.io/) [Source 2](https://apchenstu.github.io/mvsnerf/) [Source 3](https://abhibagul.github.io/OpenReco/) |
| Editing, captions & post-production | Researched agent-controlled video edits, AI photo adjustments and text-based media changes. Detailed guides disclose which intelligence is local and which comes from a separate model service. [Source 1](https://www.denrakeiw.com/scumble) [Source 2](https://kokon.app/) [Source 3](https://subtitleedit.github.io/subtitleedit/) |
| Vector graphics, illustration & textures | DesigNet supplies a concrete neural glyph-to-editable-SVG workflow. Exported outlines remain drafts requiring typography and geometry review. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06494) [Source 2](https://github.com/TomasGuija/DesigNet) |
| Typography, fonts & layout | Searched learned font generation and AI-assisted font editors. DesigNet received source/license review; Counterpunch and Makefont product descriptions did not alone establish eligible released software. [Source 1](https://counterpunch.space/about/) [Source 2](https://makefont.ai/what-is-makefont) [Source 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06494) |

StorySprout and collaborative-comic research provided primary context, while OpenManga’s mock and real-art sample modes were distinguished. [Source 1](https://story-sprout.org/research/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17263) [Source 3](https://github.com/pr0h0/OpenManga) |

An advertised restoration repository had empty main.py and Program.cs files, so no implemented tool was established despite its MIT file. [Source 1](https://autoshade.dev/) [Source 2](https://kokon.app/) [Source 3](https://github.com/GridMayorTell/photo-restoration-enhancer) |

Datoviz is relevant rendering infrastructure, but a conventional renderer is not automatically an AI tool. [Source 1](https://datoviz.org/) [Source 2](https://nodetool.ai/) |

| Fashion, textiles & wearable media | Reviewed garment generation and editable sewing-pattern research. AIpparel, ChatGarment and EasyFashion are primary findings, but a paper alone does not clear implementation licensing or manufacturing accuracy. BrainArt has a concrete prototype interface and neural image-generation call; this is not evidence of mind-reading or reliable emotion inference. [Source 1](https://var-mar.info/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/vinci2023-49.pdf) [Source 2](https://github.com/Union-Neurotech/BrainArt) |

ShaderAgent’s code is MIT, while its bundled preview scenes and optional learned scorer have noncommercial terms that the guide separates. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14988) [Source 2](https://github.com/VAST-AI-Research/ShaderAgent/blob/main/NOTICE) |

University project pages supplied primary findings; sensor requirements and hardware compatibility are not inferred from rendered images. OrigamiCP and COrigami supplied research records; no newly reviewed end-to-end paper fabrication tool was established. GardenDesigner, TerraForge and EcoDesign supplied primary leads, with full implementation/license validation still incomplete for new additions. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01777) [Source 2](https://terraforge.art/) [Source 3](https://ecodesign.earthunited.eco/) |

Mosaic Template Studio adds a screened local-CUNet template workflow with artist/printer acceptance gaps; missing acceptance evidence keeps quality unverified. Kerfline’s modified Apache-form grant remains held for clarification. [Source 1](https://www.kerfline.build/) [Source 2](https://github.com/rainesdrew/kerfline/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 3](https://github.com/mattkoczwara/template-studio) |

Primary examples and papers were found; unchanged already-known work is not republished as a new release. [Source 1](https://diffusionillusions.com/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06186) |
| AI relief engraving and learned halftoning | Searched learned halftoning and image-to-mark synthesis. The CVPR supplement provides primary evaluation context; Deep-Halftoning explicitly reports non-reproducible paper numbers and remains an unfinished research lead. [Source 1](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/CVPR2024/supplemental/Jiang_Ink_Dot-Oriented_Differentiable_CVPR_2024_supplemental.pdf) [Source 2](https://github.com/Nikronic/Deep-Halftoning) |
| Neural brushstroke painting and drawn media | Covered learned stroke composition and neural painting. Primary papers supplied algorithmic/creative findings; a paper’s artwork is not an independent test of a separately released implementation. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.03504) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2011.08114) |
| AI color direction and palette-based media | Searched neural presets and automatic color direction. NeuralPreset’s primary project page was researched; current availability, software licensing and model requirements need separate confirmation for any new profile. [Source 1](https://ZHKKKe.github.io/NeuralPreset) |
| AI collage, cut-paper composition and layered media | Covered reinforcement-learning collage and AI-driven layouts. CollageRL’s actual CC BY-NC software file excludes it despite a relevant paper; ordinary particle collage without AI is also outside scope. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.02202) [Source 2](https://github.com/northadventure/CollageRL) |
| AI stereo conversion and spatial video authoring | Researched learned stereo and headset media. VR3D Studio received a scoped guide; model-size licenses differ, and depth-based stereo conversion does not recover genuine missing viewpoints. [Source 1](https://elastic3d.github.io/) [Source 2](https://video-eye2eye.github.io/) [Source 3](https://rendepth.com/) |
| AI semantic editing of captured 3D scenes | Searched language/semantic edits to Gaussian and neural scenes. VF-Editor supplies primary context; generated scene edits still need geometry and view-consistency inspection. [Source 1](https://qinbaigao.github.io/VF-Editor-project-page/) |
| AI signing avatars and expressive gesture media | Covered learned signing and editable avatar playback. MMS Player was examined but its documented operation is procedural animation of supplied sign streams, so a direct AI component was not established for inclusion. [Source 1](https://iet.open.ac.uk/projects/signature-bsl) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07974) [Source 3](https://github.com/DFKI-SignLanguage/MMS-Player) |
| AI assisted audio plugin and DSP authoring | Researched agent-aided audio engines and learned DSP. WaveCauldron’s actual model request path was inspected: Claude supplies an editable recipe and a local DSP engine renders it. Ruse’s already-completed guide is not recycled unchanged. [Source 1](https://www.symbia-labs.com/ruse/) [Source 2](https://audioascode.com/) [Source 3](https://github.com/ManuelFCastillo/WaveCauldron/blob/main/src/ai/brew.ts) |
| AI panoramic worlds and virtual tour creation | New adaptive field: AI panorama generation, virtual tours and explorable environments. OmniRoam uses research-only software terms. An advertised panorama generator was held after source inspection found missing required modules. Cubric’s workflow is context rather than proof of a newly eligible tool. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30045) [Source 2](https://cubric.studio/ai-360-panorama-generator/) [Source 3](https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam) [Source 4](https://github.com/Memorytriassess/ai-panorama-360-generator) |

## Search and review counts

66 creative fields; 531/531 repository queries attempted; 18/18 model-task queries attempted. 25709 distinct source candidates and 855 model leads. 67 detailed profiles and 17 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 1 failed repository queries, 0 partial repository queries, 113 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 2 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: Live GitLab search and the primary Samplebrain project were inspected. The accessible project description did not establish a newly eligible creative-AI tool; no GitLab-wide absence is claimed. [Source](https://gitlab.com/then-try-this/samplebrain)
- **codeberg** · gap: Site-scoped live research and direct access encountered robots/access restrictions. Coverage is incomplete, not evidence that no eligible projects exist.
- **sourcehut** · gap: Live git.sr.ht research encountered robots/access restrictions. No complete ecosystem coverage or negative finding is claimed.
- **packages** · searched: Researched PyPI creative-audio/agent packages and followed package references to primary source documentation. A package listing alone did not replace complete software-license review. [Source](https://pypi.org/project/foley/0.0.17/) [Source](https://audioascode.com/) [Source](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Researched Krita installation documentation, Blender bridges and Unity agent tools. Required DCC/engine hosts, models and services remain separately identified. [Source](https://docs.interstice.cloud/installation/) [Source](https://github.com/shefq/blendrelay-mcp) [Source](https://github.com/Besty0728/Unity-Skills)
- **project-sites** · searched: Primary project-site research covered local editing, audio, immersive worlds, CAD and experimental media. New eligible entries were checked against actual source files and complete grants. [Source](https://www.denrakeiw.com/scumble) [Source](https://kokon.app/) [Source](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source](https://cubric.studio/ai-360-panorama-generator/)
- **research-code** · searched: Primary papers and linked implementations covered typography, holography, motion, acoustic scenes, sensory media and material generation. Missing code, missing checkpoints and restrictive software grants remain explicit gaps. [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06494) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30045) [Source](https://hapticgen.hcitech.org/) [Source](https://www.computationalimaging.org/publications/neuralholography3d/)
- **international** · searched: Reviewed Chinese and Vietnamese creative-software documentation, multilingual speech tools and Japanese CAD-agent material. Hardware/platform and licensing rules were applied equally across languages. [Source](https://github.com/potoior/CapsuleCast) [Source](https://app.danghuuson.com/vieneu-audio-studio/) [Source](https://github.com/dduongtrandai/LA-Studio) [Source](https://github.com/rsasaki0109/MusubiCAD)

## RapidRetouch

Photography, restoration & color · Images & design · Editing, captions & post-production

First complete library guide for a repository created within the current search window; creation date is not proof of a product launch.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Retouch portraits, isolate backdrops and batch-export TIFF/JPEG while preserving original photos.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-24. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README links a maintainer tutorial and describes portrait workflows; output fidelity has not been independently assessed here. [Source](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Choose the Windows installer, Apple Silicon DMG, or Linux AppImage/deb/rpm from Releases. First launch downloads the engine; models download on first use. Installers are unsigned. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The maintainer identifies BiRefNet HR as MIT and LaMa/MediaPipe as Apache-2.0. These are separately downloaded models; software AGPL terms do not substitute for their terms. Local compute; no inference account or per-image credits documented. [Source](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The glasses-reflection removal feature is alpha; conventional retouching sliders do not redraw faces. No local testing or independent reproduction was performed.

### How it uses AI

Local portrait retouching combines learned subject/face masks and LaMa object removal with conventional, nondestructive photographic adjustments. [Source](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### Introduction

Local portrait retouching combines learned subject/face masks and LaMa object removal with conventional, nondestructive photographic adjustments. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### What it is good for

Retouch portraits, isolate backdrops and batch-export TIFF/JPEG while preserving original photos. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README links a maintainer tutorial and describes portrait workflows; output fidelity has not been independently assessed here. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### Install

Choose the Windows installer, Apple Silicon DMG, or Linux AppImage/deb/rpm from Releases. First launch downloads the engine; models download on first use. Installers are unsigned. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Choose the Windows installer, Apple Silicon DMG, or Linux AppImage/deb/rpm from Releases. First launch downloads the engine; models download on first use. Installers are unsigned.
### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

1. Open a portrait and let the subject/face models load.
2. Adjust skin, backdrop or removal controls; compare before/after and inspect fine texture.
3. Save a preset if useful, then export a copy as TIFF or JPEG.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

- **Hardware:** First-run engine download about 3 GB for NVIDIA, about 1 GB otherwise; disk about 7 GB for NVIDIA or 2–3 GB otherwise, plus model downloads. RAM/VRAM minimums are not documented.
- **Software:** Packaged releases; source development specifies Node 20+, Rust, uv, Python 3.12 and Tauri 2 system packages.
- **Platforms:** Windows 64-bit; Apple Silicon Macs, excluding Intel; Linux amd64. CPU inference is documented, with NVIDIA or Apple GPU acceleration where supported.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

- **Code:** AGPL-3.0
- **Weights:** The maintainer identifies BiRefNet HR as MIT and LaMa/MediaPipe as Apache-2.0. These are separately downloaded models; software AGPL terms do not substitute for their terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows AGPL-3.0. The maintainer identifies BiRefNet HR as MIT and LaMa/MediaPipe as Apache-2.0. These are separately downloaded models; software AGPL terms do not substitute for their terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute; no inference account or per-image credits documented.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### Limitations

The glasses-reflection removal feature is alpha; conventional retouching sliders do not redraw faces. No local testing or independent reproduction was performed. [Source 1](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/crispin81/RapidRetouch/blob/master/README.md)

## Scumble

Images & design · Photography, restoration & color · Editing, captions & post-production

First complete guide in this library, including any earlier screened entry. This is new library coverage, not a claim that the software launched today.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Inpaint selected regions, compose multiple generated layers and export editable image work.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-24. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The current 0.1.x editor is described as young with rough edges, and its live-testing section identifies unfinished end-to-end validation across supported routes. https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Four maintainer walkthrough videos and an offline manual are linked; videos establish examples, not an independent quality verdict. [Source](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Install the Windows Store copy or obtain the GitHub installer/portable archive. For local generation, install the companion InpaintCanvas node in an existing ComfyUI and connect its address; alternatively configure a supported provider key. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** GPL-3.0 app code; fonts and model recipes carry separate notices. Local checkpoint terms and paid model-provider terms vary by recipe. Local compute for ComfyUI or separate API/cloud charges; neither is included in the app license. [Source](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The maintainer calls the app young with rough edges and says several API routes and Linux builds lack end-to-end testing. Keep project backups and verify the selected route.

### How it uses AI

A layered image editor sends selections and prompts to ComfyUI or model APIs and returns editable, color-matched image layers. [Source](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A layered image editor sends selections and prompts to ComfyUI or model APIs and returns editable, color-matched image layers. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Inpaint selected regions, compose multiple generated layers and export editable image work. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

Four maintainer walkthrough videos and an offline manual are linked; videos establish examples, not an independent quality verdict. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install the Windows Store copy or obtain the GitHub installer/portable archive. For local generation, install the companion InpaintCanvas node in an existing ComfyUI and connect its address; alternatively configure a supported provider key. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the Windows Store copy or obtain the GitHub installer/portable archive. For local generation, install the companion InpaintCanvas node in an existing ComfyUI and connect its address; alternatively configure a supported provider key.

```sh
winget install scumble
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

1. Open an image, select a region or draw a layout box.
2. Choose a model recipe and connection, describe the edit, then generate.
3. Inspect the new layer, adjust its match/mask/blend and save or export.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Windows 10/11 64-bit; local generation additionally needs ComfyUI and the matching custom node/models. Source development uses npm install and npm start.
- **Platforms:** Windows is the documented working route. Linux AppImage/deb builds exist but were not run by the author; macOS is planned.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/DenRakEiw/scumble/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** GPL-3.0
- **Weights:** GPL-3.0 app code; fonts and model recipes carry separate notices. Local checkpoint terms and paid model-provider terms vary by recipe.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows GPL-3.0. GPL-3.0 app code; fonts and model recipes carry separate notices. Local checkpoint terms and paid model-provider terms vary by recipe.

- **Software:** Windows 10 64-bit or newer; macOS 15+; Linux Ubuntu 22.04/Debian 12/Mint 21/Fedora 40 or newer. Drivers and model runtimes depend on hardware.
- **Platforms:** Windows NVIDIA is the main documented path. Apple Silicon Metal, Linux and AMD/Intel Vulkan paths are beta; Intel Macs are unsupported.

A local studio joins image/text-to-3D models with mesh cleanup, UV generation, material baking and an agent-callable MCP server. [Source](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A local studio joins image/text-to-3D models with mesh cleanup, UV generation, material baking and an agent-callable MCP server. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Prototype textured game assets from reference images on Windows/Linux NVIDIA machines. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The repository documents a viewer and exports; mock-mode previews use procedural placeholders and are not AI generation evidence. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Set up the project with the platform setup/dev scripts, then separately install the CUDA PyTorch stack and a supported model backend. The initial UI runs in mock mode until real models are installed. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Set up the project with the platform setup/dev scripts, then separately install the CUDA PyTorch stack and a supported model backend. The initial UI runs in mock mode until real models are installed.

```sh
./scripts/setup.sh
```


```sh
./scripts/dev.sh
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

1. Open the local studio and confirm whether the engine is real or mock.
2. Select a supported checkpoint, supply an image or prompt and generate one asset.
3. Inspect geometry/UVs, adjust target complexity and export; use Blender for FBX.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Real generation requires NVIDIA CUDA. The documented model table gives approximate model-specific VRAM, including about 6 GB for TripoSR; RAM and total disk minimums are not specified.
- **Software:** Node 18+, uv-managed Python 3.11, CUDA/PyTorch and backend-specific dependencies; Blender for FBX export.
- **Platforms:** Windows NVIDIA is the primary tested route; Linux NVIDIA is documented. macOS and non-NVIDIA machines only have mock-mode geometry in this project.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT project code; TRELLIS, TripoSR, Hunyuan and SDXL model terms differ and require separate review before commercial work.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT project code; TRELLIS, TripoSR, Hunyuan and SDXL model terms differ and require separate review before commercial work.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Several PBR maps are image-processing estimates, not recovered physical materials. Generated meshes are not automatically game-ready or printable. [Source 1](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/felippeomgt/mymeshy/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Paper and default-config models differ. Local rendering, scoring and API calls can be expensive; a successful graph is not proof of faithful material recovery.

Multiple vision-language agents convert reference photos into editable Blender material node graphs, then iteratively render and score candidates. ### Introduction

Multiple vision-language agents convert reference photos into editable Blender material node graphs, then iteratively render and score candidates. 2. Clone the source, install requirements and put src on PYTHONPATH. Configure Blender, a vision-capable chat endpoint and model access in the YAML before running the supplied example.

```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
python -m shader_agent --config config/default.yaml --image data/xmas.png --text "The wrapper of the present box." --output outputs/xmas
```

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/VAST-AI-Research/ShaderAgent/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/VAST-AI-Research/ShaderAgent/blob/main/NOTICE)

- **Platforms:** The documented path requires NVIDIA rendering; macOS/AMD compatibility is not established by the browser or Python components.

## CapsuleCast

First complete library guide for a repository created within the current search window; creation date is not proof of a product launch.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Scope: Template-based short explainers and narrated visual lessons with editable project stages.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-24. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/potoior/CapsuleCast/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README links a maintainer introduction MP4 and actual rendered scene frames; these do not establish reliability for arbitrary topics. [Source](https://github.com/potoior/CapsuleCast/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Install Python 3.12+, Node 20+, Docker and Chrome. Start the documented PostgreSQL/Redis infrastructure, install API and web dependencies, then configure the model gateway and Chrome path in local environment files. Run npm install inside apps/web, then return to the repository root; start make api, make worker and make web in separate terminals. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/potoior/CapsuleCast/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT project code. LLM/TTS weights and gateways have separate terms; the rendering stack also depends on GSAP under its own license. Qwen3-TTS, Edge TTS and Kokoro are distinct speech routes. Local infrastructure plus any selected LLM/TTS service charges. [Source](https://github.com/potoior/CapsuleCast/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Template layouts can produce small text or overflow. A local gateway is optional, and a cloud gateway sends content to its provider. No automatic social posting is included in this review.

- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the repository with Node 22+ and FFmpeg available, install dependencies, build and open a local footage folder. The first render downloads Chrome Headless Shell. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. - **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT application code; Remotion has separate terms, with a company license required above the documented free tier. Optional HyperFrames/GSAP, Whisper weights, cloud hosting and image services retain separate terms. Local editing/transcription/rendering plus applicable Remotion licensing; optional Cloudflare and OpenRouter charges. [Source](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Cloudflare sharing is optional and exposes source clips on published watch pages. Optional image generation uses an external API. A responsive phone editor does not mean rendering runs on the phone.

### How it uses AI

A browser timeline and agent CLI use local Whisper transcripts and structured cut lists to assemble footage, captions and vertical shorts. [Source](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

### Introduction

A browser timeline and agent CLI use local Whisper transcripts and structured cut lists to assemble footage, captions and vertical shorts. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

### What it is good for

Turning a folder of clips into a full cut and short version while keeping the edit inspectable. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

### Demo & examples

The official explainer is stated to have been edited in the app; screenshots show timeline, captions and watch pages. Independent use has not been established here. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

### Install

Clone the repository with Node 22+ and FFmpeg available, install dependencies, build and open a local footage folder. The first render downloads Chrome Headless Shell. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the repository with Node 22+ and FFmpeg available, install dependencies, build and open a local footage folder. The first render downloads Chrome Headless Shell.

```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm run build
```


```sh
node bin/videdit.mjs open /path/to/clips
```


```sh
videdit transcribe /path/to/clips
```


```sh
videdit render /path/to/clips --timeline Short
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

1. Import clips and generate word timings.
2. Review an agent-authored cut list or edit the browser timeline.
3. Render a local test and inspect framing/captions before optionally configuring sharing.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Node 22+, FFmpeg, Remotion and headless Chrome; first Chrome download is about 170 MB.
- **Platforms:** A browser UI and local Node setup are documented. The README uses Mac examples but does not specify a complete supported-host/version matrix.

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/veiovi/video-bitedit/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YY0tsgIJ2bo)

- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT application code; Remotion has separate terms, with a company license required above the documented free tier. Optional HyperFrames/GSAP, Whisper weights, cloud hosting and image services retain separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local editing/transcription/rendering plus applicable Remotion licensing; optional Cloudflare and OpenRouter charges.

It explicitly says no real user footage was edited in those checks; synthetic tests do not establish creative editing quality. [Source](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the source checkout with uv and FFmpeg/ffprobe. Direct setup installs the editor integration for supported agent clients; run its doctor before selecting media. Native Final Cut setup is separate. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT original code; optional OpenMontage is an independently licensed AGPL-3.0 submodule. Agent access, legacy Gemini/ElevenLabs APIs and Final Cut Pro have separate terms and costs. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Synthetic-media tests and schema validation do not prove transcription, pacing or live editing of arbitrary footage. The Final Cut controller needs the app frontmost; do not conflate its permission requirements with direct editing.

### How it uses AI

An agent-controlled Python editor turns selected footage into an MP4 through transcription, edit decisions and render review; a separate Mac controller operates Final Cut Pro. [Source](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

An agent-controlled Python editor turns selected footage into an MP4 through transcription, edit decisions and render review; a separate Mac controller operates Final Cut Pro. [Source 1](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

A reviewable direct-edit workflow for selected clips, with an optional native Final Cut integration. [Source 1](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README reports generated-media acceptance tests and a separate Final Cut canary. It explicitly says no real user footage was edited in those checks; synthetic tests do not establish creative editing quality. [Source 1](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Use the source checkout with uv and FFmpeg/ffprobe. Direct setup installs the editor integration for supported agent clients; run its doctor before selecting media. Native Final Cut setup is separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the source checkout with uv and FFmpeg/ffprobe. Direct setup installs the editor integration for supported agent clients; run its doctor before selecting media. Native Final Cut setup is separate.

```sh
uv run editor-cli direct setup
```


```sh
uv run editor-cli direct doctor
```


```sh
uv run editor-cli direct start --file /path/to/clip.mp4 --prompt "Make a short captioned edit"
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Screddyice/editor-cli/blob/main/README.md)

1. Select the exact source files and describe the edit.
2. Review the proposed editing strategy and rendered preview.
3. Inspect the final MP4; the separate Final Cut workflow leaves master export to the editor.

- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT original code; optional OpenMontage is an independently licensed AGPL-3.0 submodule. Agent access, legacy Gemini/ElevenLabs APIs and Final Cut Pro have separate terms and costs.

### How it uses AI

A diffusion model predicts Gaussian splats from an image for 3D object generation and scene reconstruction, with documented mesh export. [Source](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

### Introduction

A diffusion model predicts Gaussian splats from an image for 3D object generation and scene reconstruction, with documented mesh export. [Source 1](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

### What it is good for

Exploring image-conditioned 3D assets and novel scene views in a research workflow. [Source 1](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

### Demo & examples

The project page and README show rotating objects, scenes and mesh examples. This open implementation is explicitly retrained and differs from the original Adobe version. [Source 1](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

### Install

Create the documented Python 3.11 environment, install PyTorch 2.5.1/torchvision 0.20.1, requirements and the rasterization/KNN extensions, then obtain the matching checkpoints. [Source 1](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Create the documented Python 3.11 environment, install PyTorch 2.5.1/torchvision 0.20.1, requirements and the rasterization/KNN extensions, then obtain the matching checkpoints.

```sh
conda create -n diffusiongs python=3.11
```


```sh
pip install torch==2.5.1 torchvision==0.20.1
```


```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
pip install -e submodules/diff-gaussian-rasterization
```


```sh
pip install -e submodules/simple-knn
```


```sh
python run.py
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/caiyuanhao1998/Open-DiffusionGS/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://caiyuanhao1998.github.io/project/DiffusionGS/)

2. Inspect multiple generated views and exported geometry.
3. For scene evaluation, prepare the required posed dataset and enable saved intermediate videos in the configuration.

### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/SifuInTheShell/Effigy/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/SifuInTheShell/Effigy/blob/main/SETUP.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/SifuInTheShell/Effigy/blob/main/licenses.yaml) [Source 4](https://github.com/SifuInTheShell/Effigy/blob/main/blender_ops/LICENSE)

- **Hardware:** NVIDIA 16 GB reference target; 12 GB Fast mode is best effort. RAM 32 GB recommended with about 20 GB host-memory peaks reported. Disk roughly 70–160 GB including models.
- **Software:** Windows 11; NVIDIA driver 575+ for the primary route; bundled Python 3.12 and Blender 5.2.2. Linux source setup requires rebuilding the kernel wheels.
- **Platforms:** Windows first; Linux from source. No macOS support. AMD RX 9070/9070 XT supports only the Fast chain, not all character/motion workflows.

The map has English/Western bias and is constrained by its synthesizer. The optional expressive backend is explicitly experimental, but that does not classify the default route.

### How it uses AI

A neural embedding model maps text prompts to a curated procedural-audio configuration, producing editable ambient soundscapes locally. [Source](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

### Introduction

A neural embedding model maps text prompts to a curated procedural-audio configuration, producing editable ambient soundscapes locally. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

### What it is good for

Sketching ambient music and interactive sound moods with explicit synthesis controls on a CPU. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

### Demo & examples

The official demo and Colab notebook provide listening examples. The retrieval-map card explains that CLAP was used both in selection and evaluation, so those scores are not independent listening evidence. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

### Install

For the full demo use the documented Docker Compose build; local SDK installation supports Python 3.11/3.12 on macOS/Linux. Prefetch the default embedding model and run doctor before generating. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. For the full demo use the documented Docker Compose build; local SDK installation supports Python 3.11/3.12 on macOS/Linux. Prefetch the default embedding model and run doctor before generating.

```sh
docker compose -f demo/docker-compose.yml up --build
```


```sh
pip install latentscore
```


```sh
latentscore doctor --strict --offline
```


```sh
latentscore download fast
```


```sh
latentscore demo --duration 30 --output ambient.wav
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

1. Try a short scene-description prompt in the demo or SDK.
2. Adjust tempo, density, brightness and other MusicConfig controls.
3. Listen to the rendered WAV and compare alternatives before integrating it into a project.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://latentscore.com/demo) [Source 3](https://github.com/prabal-rje/latentscore/blob/main/RETRIEVAL_MAP_CARD.md)

- **Hardware:** CPU-only; no GPU required. Default model download about 90 MB; optional CLAP about 1.8 GB. Minimum RAM/total disk are not stated.
- **Software:** Docker Compose for the full UI, or Python 3.11/3.12 with platform audio libraries for the SDK. The optional expressive local-LLM extra is not part of this default workflow.
- **Platforms:** Docker demo documented on Linux, Mac and Windows with WSL2. Native SDK supports Mac/Linux; native Windows and WSL2 pip are explicitly unsupported.

FFmpeg and other components keep their own terms. Local compute and model downloads; no required inference API. [Source](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Monocular stereo synthesis can expose holes, depth errors and temporal artifacts. VR180 projection does not reconstruct unseen scene geometry or establish viewer comfort. Headset coverage is a maintainer claim.

### How it uses AI

Depth Anything V2 estimates monocular depth and drives stereoscopic warping to convert photos or video into SBS or VR180-style output. [Source](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

### Introduction

Depth Anything V2 estimates monocular depth and drives stereoscopic warping to convert photos or video into SBS or VR180-style output. [Source 1](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

### What it is good for

Previewing depth-based stereo conversions of existing media and rendering short headset test clips. [Source 1](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

### Demo & examples

The README documents a wiggle preview and a five-second headset sample workflow. Its speed and compatibility claims have not been independently tested here. [Source 1](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

### Install

Clone grezoo/vr3d-studio, install the requirements plus a matching CUDA PyTorch build, place FFmpeg on PATH and launch main.py. Selected depth weights download automatically. [Source 1](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone grezoo/vr3d-studio, install the requirements plus a matching CUDA PyTorch build, place FFmpeg on PATH and launch main.py. Selected depth weights download automatically.

```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
python main.py
```


```sh
python main.py -i input_video.mp4 -m vr180 --fov 110
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/grezoo/vr3d-studio/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/DepthAnything/Depth-Anything-V2)

2. Choose model, stereo strength and convergence; inspect the wiggle preview.

### Introduction

An AI agent can plan a JSON edit decision list from media observations, which this toolchain applies to an editable Blender Video Sequencer timeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

### What it is good for

Reviewable agent-assisted assembly with captions, A/B-roll and audio operations while preserving an editable timeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

### Demo & examples

The README provides a minimal EDL and links validation/practice records. Its sample footage is a placeholder, not a bundled end-to-end creative demo. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

### Install

Install the release add-on ZIP in Blender 5.2.0 LTS. Keep the external Python 3.13 perception environment separate, install the Windows lockfile dependencies and provide FFmpeg/FFprobe. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the release add-on ZIP in Blender 5.2.0 LTS. Keep the external Python 3.13 perception environment separate, install the Windows lockfile dependencies and provide FFmpeg/FFprobe.

```sh
python -m venv .venv
```


```sh
python -m pip install -r requirements-win-py313.lock
```


```sh
python vse_perceive.py probe input.mp4
```


```sh
python vse_perceive.py sample input.mp4 --step 15 --outdir out/sample
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

1. Inspect the source media and have your chosen agent propose an EDL.
2. Review the EDL, real file paths and dry-run output before explicitly approving execution.
3. Save a separate Blender project and inspect source frames, subtitles and sound before export.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Blender 5.2.0 LTS and a separate Python 3.13 environment; FFmpeg/FFprobe. Optional transcription downloads a speech model and GPU use needs extra runtime libraries.
- **Platforms:** Tested Windows configuration only. The supplied dependency lock is not a Mac/Linux compatibility promise.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 4](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

- **Code:** GPL-3.0
- **Weights:** GPL-3.0-or-later project code. Blender, media libraries, speech weights and any external agent/model retain separate terms; no decision LLM is bundled.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows GPL-3.0. GPL-3.0-or-later project code. Blender, media libraries, speech weights and any external agent/model retain separate terms; no decision LLM is bundled.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

### Limitations

The maintainer identifies unresolved adjacent-frame decoding differences; frame-exact deliveries need manual verification. Agent approval and synthetic fixture success do not establish visual correctness. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/NOTICE) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lijiahong77/blender-vse/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)

## Dual-kernel CAD agent harness

3D printing & generative CAD · AI parametric design and jewelry · 3D, reconstruction & assets

First complete library guide for a repository created within the current search window; creation date is not proof of a product launch.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Checking simple parametric part geometry before exporting STEP, STL or technical drawings.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-24. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README includes plate, bracket and flange seeds with volume/bounds comparison output; the values are maintainer examples rather than independent manufacturing validation. [Source](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Create the Python 3.13 build123d environment with uv and install the listed packages. The optional SMLib route needs a separate compatible Python environment. Set the documented interpreter paths before launching the verifier or MCP server. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT harness; the README identifies build123d as Apache-2.0, smlibpy as MIT/Apache/OFL and OCCT as LGPL with exception. These dependency claims need version-specific review; the agent/model is separate. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Agreement on volume, bounds and solid validity is limited evidence, not proof of every geometric or fabrication property. Single-kernel use cannot provide the advertised independent-kernel cross-check.

### How it uses AI

An MCP interface lets AI agents model parts and compare their measured geometry against independent CAD kernels and hand-calculated expectations. [Source](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

An MCP interface lets AI agents model parts and compare their measured geometry against independent CAD kernels and hand-calculated expectations. [Source 1](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Checking simple parametric part geometry before exporting STEP, STL or technical drawings. [Source 1](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README includes plate, bracket and flange seeds with volume/bounds comparison output; the values are maintainer examples rather than independent manufacturing validation. [Source 1](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Create the Python 3.13 build123d environment with uv and install the listed packages. The optional SMLib route needs a separate compatible Python environment. Set the documented interpreter paths before launching the verifier or MCP server. [Source 1](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Create the Python 3.13 build123d environment with uv and install the listed packages. The optional SMLib route needs a separate compatible Python environment. Set the documented interpreter paths before launching the verifier or MCP server.

```sh
uv venv --python 3.13 .venv
```


```sh
uv pip install build123d ezdxf trimesh cairosvg mcp
```


```sh
.venv/bin/python verify/verify.py
```


```sh
.venv/bin/python mcp/server.py
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/w23x0/cad/blob/main/README.md)

1. Start with a supplied small part and independently derived dimensions/expected volume.
2. Ask the agent to model, measure and inspect views before exporting.
3. Use both configured kernels for cross-checking; review tolerances and manufacturing constraints separately.

npm run doctor
```

npm run verify:models
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Old-Doggy/lenssort-ai/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Old-Doggy/lenssort-ai/blob/main/docs/model-sources.md)

```sh
python scripts/discover_environment.py --pretty
```


```sh
python scripts/inspect_media.py input.mp4
```


```sh
python scripts/auto_edit.py input.mp4 --preset 9:16 --framing blur_fill --max-duration 30 --output-plan plan.json
```


```sh
python scripts/validate_edit_plan.py plan.json
```


```sh
python scripts/render_engine.py plan.json
```

An AI agent writes editable MusicXML and uses a Python toolkit to engrave and export it through MuseScore; a companion route assists score-scan transcription. ## Difix-Turbo training implementation

First complete library guide for a repository created within the current search window; creation date is not proof of a product launch.

### Quality assessment: Experimental

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Developers researching reference-guided cleanup of NeRF or splat renders, with their own posed captures and training run.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-24. No local execution implied.

Why Experimental: The maintainer explicitly reports that the current implementation has never been trained on the available hardware and supplies no trained weights or model results yet. https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README shows a generated training triplet from phone capture. It explicitly states that this implementation has no trained weights or model results yet; the upstream paper is not evidence that this rewrite works. [Source](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the source into an isolated Python environment, install requirements and prepare posed capture/reconstruction data. Train the documented adapter before attempting checkpoint-based evaluation or refinement; there is no ready pretrained application to launch. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT implementation, with acknowledged MIT img2img-turbo-derived forward-pass ideas. SD-Turbo weights download under separate Stability AI terms; capture data and reconstruction dependencies retain their terms. Local GPU training and model/data downloads; no required hosted-inference API. [Source](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. No trained checkpoint or validated cleanup result is supplied. Producing training triplets does not establish inference quality. This guide covers the released training workflow, not a ready-to-use restoration application.

### How it uses AI

A single-step diffusion training implementation learns to clean novel-view reconstruction artifacts using a nearby real photograph as reference. [Source](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

### Introduction

A single-step diffusion training implementation learns to clean novel-view reconstruction artifacts using a nearby real photograph as reference. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

### What it is good for

Developers researching reference-guided cleanup of NeRF or splat renders, with their own posed captures and training run. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

### Demo & examples

The README shows a generated training triplet from phone capture. It explicitly states that this implementation has no trained weights or model results yet; the upstream paper is not evidence that this rewrite works. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

### Install

Clone the source into an isolated Python environment, install requirements and prepare posed capture/reconstruction data. Train the documented adapter before attempting checkpoint-based evaluation or refinement; there is no ready pretrained application to launch. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the source into an isolated Python environment, install requirements and prepare posed capture/reconstruction data. Train the documented adapter before attempting checkpoint-based evaluation or refinement; there is no ready pretrained application to launch.

```sh
pip install -r requirements.txt
```


```sh
python tools/make_pairs.py --dataset runs/car/dataset --out data/car
```


```sh
python train.py --train data/car1 data/car2 --val data/car3 --out ckpt/run1 --steps 3000
```


```sh
python evaluate.py --ckpt ckpt/run1/difix_lora.pt --data data/car3 --out results/car3
```


```sh
python refine.py --ckpt ckpt/run1/difix_lora.pt --renders renders/ --refs refs/ --out refined/
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

1. Build artifact-render/target/reference triplets from owned posed captures.
2. Train on selected captures while holding an entire different capture out for validation.
3. Inspect before/after images as well as metrics before distilling any result back into a reconstruction.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

- **Hardware:** The maintainer says training needs more than 6 GB GPU memory and could not train on a 6 GB laptop. A tested sufficient VRAM value, system RAM and disk minimum are not documented.
- **Software:** Requirements include PyTorch >=2.1, diffusers 0.31, transformers 4.46.3, peft 0.13.2, accelerate and LPIPS. A minimum Python version and complete reconstruction-backend setup are not documented.
- **Platforms:** GPU Python research workflow; a complete native platform support matrix is not published.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT implementation, with acknowledged MIT img2img-turbo-derived forward-pass ideas. SD-Turbo weights download under separate Stability AI terms; capture data and reconstruction dependencies retain their terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT implementation, with acknowledged MIT img2img-turbo-derived forward-pass ideas. SD-Turbo weights download under separate Stability AI terms; capture data and reconstruction dependencies retain their terms.
- **Cost:** Local GPU training and model/data downloads; no required hosted-inference API.

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/neelgajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/NeelGajera/difix-turbo/blob/main/requirements.txt)

### How it uses AI

An MCP server and Node library let an AI agent edit semantic 3D scene documents, inspect rendered views and geometry critiques, then export meshes, rigs, animations and effect sprites. [Source](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

### Introduction

An MCP server and Node library let an AI agent edit semantic 3D scene documents, inspect rendered views and geometry critiques, then export meshes, rigs, animations and effect sprites. [Source 1](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

### What it is good for

Agent-assisted stylized game props and characters with editable dimensions, part relationships, animation clips and glTF exports. [Source 1](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

### Demo & examples

The README provides rendered template galleries, a goblin walk sequence, fire flipbooks and a linked Cargo Century showcase. These are maintainer examples, not independent benchmarking. [Source 1](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

### Install

Install Node 20+ and connect the package command to an MCP-capable assistant. The README documents npm/npx, fixed-release tarballs and source checkout routes. The assistant/model is supplied separately. [Source 1](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install Node 20+ and connect the package command to an MCP-capable assistant. The README documents npm/npx, fixed-release tarballs and source checkout routes. The assistant/model is supplied separately.

```sh
npx -y @s1444/clayform mcp
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/sezginkipel/clayform/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://clayform.sadelabs.site)

1. Connect the MCP server and choose a template.
2. Ask the assistant for a small semantic edit to a part, material or attachment.
3. Inspect the multi-view render, measured geometry and named critique findings.
4. Preview a motion clip and export GLB, OBJ or a sprite flipbook.

### How it uses AI

A Forge extension uses optional neural subject matting to extract performers into frame layers that form navigable temporal sculptures; optional Deforum generation transforms their appearance. [Source](https://github.com/Shai7net/sd-forge-time-strata/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Forge extension uses optional neural subject matting to extract performers into frame layers that form navigable temporal sculptures; optional Deforum generation transforms their appearance. [Source 1](https://github.com/Shai7net/sd-forge-time-strata/blob/main/README.md)

In an existing Forge-family installation, use Extensions → Install from URL with the repository URL, then fully stop and restart Forge. Optional AI matting packages and models use Forge’s own Python environment. [Source 1](https://github.com/Shai7net/sd-forge-time-strata/blob/main/README.md)

2. Choose neural matting or an existing alpha channel, build the chain and inspect difficult frames.
3. Arrange layers along a path and set the camera keys.
4. Record MP4 or WebM while keeping the page open; retain uploads and prepared chains with the project.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Shai7net/sd-forge-time-strata/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** WebGL2-capable hardware acceleration. Extraction can use ONNX CUDA with CPU fallback; NVENC additionally needs a supported NVIDIA device/driver/encoder. No numeric minimum RAM, VRAM or storage is specified.
- **Software:** A working Forge host and its Python environment; a WebGL2 browser with hardware acceleration; NumPy, Pillow, OpenCV and imageio-ffmpeg. Neural matting adds ONNX Runtime/rembg or manually supplied RVM assets.
- **Platforms:** Host compatibility is feature-dependent. The developer documents Windows folder integration and manual paths on Linux/macOS, with core CI on Windows/Linux; that does not establish full host renders on each OS or Forge variant.

2. Use Node 18+ in the source checkout, install dependencies, copy the example environment configuration and start the Express server. Configure an optional Groq key for chat; set a private JWT secret rather than retaining the example value.

```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm start
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheSuraj01/TalkingHead-3D/blob/main/README.md)

1. Open the documented localhost:3000 view.
2. Send a short phrase through /api/speak with a mood and gesture.
3. Switch to the minimal service view for an overlay or embed the avatar through the documented iframe interface.
4. Add the optional LLM integration only after the basic speech and avatar path works.
