- **Weights:** Microsoft Edge neural voices, optional Google TTS, Groq-hosted models and avatar/animation assets retain their own terms. The source MIT grant does not open-source those services or license all bundled artwork.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Microsoft Edge neural voices, optional Google TTS, Groq-hosted models and avatar/animation assets retain their own terms. The source MIT grant does not open-source those services or license all bundled artwork.

### How it uses AI

An Android tablet modeler turns a prompt into validated shape JSON through Claude, then lets a user arrange, edit and export a toy-like 3D design. ### Introduction

An Android tablet modeler turns a prompt into validated shape JSON through Claude, then lets a user arrange, edit and export a toy-like 3D design. ### Install

Build with JDK 17+ and Android SDK/build-tools 35 using the documented Gradle project; install the resulting APK on Android 8+. Release signing needs a configured keystore. Set the provider through the app’s adult settings or a private proxy, not a key embedded in a public APK. [Source 1](https://github.com/elcolumbio/toyforge/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/elcolumbio/toyforge/blob/master/THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Build with JDK 17+ and Android SDK/build-tools 35 using the documented Gradle project; install the resulting APK on Android 8+. Release signing needs a configured keystore. Set the provider through the app’s adult settings or a private proxy, not a key embedded in a public APK.

```sh
./gradlew assembleDebug
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/elcolumbio/toyforge/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/elcolumbio/toyforge/blob/master/THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md)

1. Create a starter toy or add a few primitive shapes.
2. Enable the optional live AI provider in the adult settings, then enter a simple design request.
3. Edit proportions, colors and placement and inspect thin/floating-part warnings.
4. Export STL or the Bambu/Orca-oriented 3MF and inspect it in the target slicer before any physical print.

- **Software:** Build: JDK 17+ (developer tested OpenJDK 21), Android SDK platform/build-tools 35. Runtime: Kotlin/Compose app using OpenGL ES 2.0, with optional Anthropic access.
- **Platforms:** Android 8.0+ tablet app is documented; desktop hosts are used for building and downstream slicers, not native ToyForge applications.

## Additional open-source AI discoveries

Creative AI relevance and software license screened. Full installation, requirements and quality profiles are pending.

### ForgeAudio neural worker · Apache-2.0

Exploring neural vocal extraction alongside a Rust/REAPER processing workflow.
The released Python worker actually calls Mel-Band-RoFormer separation and optional de-reverberation through audio-separator; a separate branch calls a hosted YuE2 song model.
Full setup, hardware, model-term and output-quality review pending. Apache-2.0 original source. The inspected worker returns vocals and an instrumental/other stem, with drums/bass/guitar/keys left empty; its header advertises Apollo/transcription tasks that are not implemented in that CLI. Model terms, Python environment, GPU requirements and integration reproduction need further review. REAPER and optional hosted generation are separate dependencies.

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **Ruach Studio current distribution** · needs-license-review: A new review found that the README identifies inherited YuE2 Kit page/scripts as lacking their own license. The full AGPL grant for original changes does not resolve that inherited-code contradiction. The earlier sealed entry is preserved, with a current library licensing warning; a complete guide is held. [Source](https://github.com/igrbible/Ruach_Studio/blob/main/README.md)
- **WorldSplat** · needs-license-review: Its Apache-form software file omits the standard separable/linking carve-out in the Derivative Works definition. The material grant change is held for clarification; the open-source label alone does not settle eligibility. [Source](https://github.com/timscheuerai/worldsplat/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Kerfline** · needs-license-review: The Apache-form grant contains textual changes beyond whitespace. The exact license interpretation remains held for clarification instead of relying on the SPDX label. [Source](https://github.com/rainesdrew/kerfline/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Modly** · needs-license-review: The license includes an additional mandatory user-interface credit requirement. Open-source eligibility of this modified grant remains unresolved. [Source](https://github.com/lightningpixel/modly/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **OmniRoam** · excluded: The released code uses Adobe research-only terms, so the panoramic-world demo does not qualify it as open-source software under this library policy. [Source](https://github.com/yuhengliu02/OmniRoam)
- **Partwright** · excluded: The inspected software grant is PolyForm Noncommercial; it does not qualify under the open-source-only scope. [Source](https://github.com/kemper/partwright)
- **AI4AnimationPy** · excluded: The current code license is CC BY-NC 4.0. Motion demonstrations do not override its noncommercial software restriction. [Source](https://github.com/facebookresearch/ai4animationpy)
- **CollageRL** · excluded: The actual code file carries CC BY-NC 4.0 terms. The relevant collage-learning research is retained as field context, not an eligible library addition. [Source](https://github.com/northadventure/CollageRL)
- **Photo Restoration Enhancer** · excluded: The inspected main.py and Program.cs files are empty; marketing pages and an external executable link do not establish an implemented open-source AI restoration application. No executable was downloaded or run. [Source](https://github.com/GridMayorTell/photo-restoration-enhancer)
- **MMS Player** · excluded: The reviewed tool synthesizes procedural avatar animation from supplied sign streams. An AI translator elsewhere in the workflow does not establish a direct AI component in this player. [Source](https://github.com/DFKI-SignLanguage/MMS-Player)
- **Vadyu MCP client** · excluded: The MIT repository is a thin bridge to a separately hosted proprietary video service. It is held out of the library under the selection policy for thin paid-API wrappers. [Source](https://github.com/crawde/mcp-vadyu)
- **AIRapVideoFree** · excluded: The source documentation describes a generic AI-site starter rather than a verified rap-video-generation implementation. The advertised creative workflow was not established. [Source](https://github.com/siuserxiaowei/airapvideofree)
- **NAVA** · needs-license-review: The license endpoint and root review did not establish a complete software grant; the project is not counted as an eligible new tool. [Source](https://github.com/ernie-research/NAVA)
- **AraMS-Restore** · needs-license-review: The license endpoint and root review did not establish a complete software grant; the project is not counted as an eligible new tool. [Source](https://github.com/ArchaText/AraMS-Restore)
- **Comic Studio** · needs-license-review: The license endpoint and root review did not establish a complete software grant; the project is not counted as an eligible new tool. [Source](https://github.com/CallmeTruong/Comic_Studio)
- **AI Architectural Design Assistant** · needs-license-review: The license endpoint and root review did not establish a complete software grant; the project is not counted as an eligible new tool. [Source](https://github.com/JMU-CS-Lykny/AI-Powered-Architectural-Design-Assistant)
- **HFG Neural Holography** · excluded: The existing screened implementation still promises release of its proposed-method checkpoint after acceptance. A reproducible full inference guide was not established today. [Source](https://github.com/HannahZhang1926/HFG-Neural-Holo)
- **Deep-Halftoning follow-up** · excluded: Current source review identifies this implementation as unfinished with non-reproduced paper numbers. Its earlier screened record remains pending; it is not recycled as a new addition. [Source](https://github.com/Nikronic/Deep-Halftoning)
- **Arabic Motion Forge** · needs-license-review: The third-party notice explicitly identifies bundled motion-reel material with no upstream license and says redistribution needs that omission resolved. The original MIT code and separately licensed AI Film Studio do not settle this distribution-wide gap. [Source](https://github.com/zoih6/arabic-motion-forge/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY.md)
- **VIDEO-DANCER** · excluded: The complete software terms reserve all rights and prohibit copying, modification and redistribution without written permission. Free use does not make this open-source software. [Source](https://github.com/koan-shdw/VIDEO-DANCER)
- **VOXELMIND-XR** · excluded: The inspected license is proprietary and does not supply an open-source software grant. [Source](https://github.com/shomratmortaza/VOXELMIND-XR)
- **WallAura** · needs-license-review: The inspected LICENSE file is empty. A README MIT badge is not a complete software grant. [Source](https://github.com/Harshgangam/WallAura---Wall-Visualization-System)
- **Hologram Parallax Photo Studio** · excluded: The reviewed source has an unfinished pipeline expression and imports absent utility modules. A neural-depth fragment exists, but the advertised complete application and executable claims were not established. No executable was downloaded or run. [Source](https://github.com/fractiongoosegrip/hologram-parallax-photo-studio/blob/main/src/hologram_studio/workers/pipeline.py)
- **AI Panorama 360 Generator** · excluded: The reviewed diffusion code imports model-cache and utility modules absent from the repository tree. The advertised complete panorama application and prebuilt executable were not established by the available source.
